Internal objective:
```
def _fft_convolution_max(p, beta=0):
    a = np.asarray(p)
    n = len(a)
    M = 1
    while M < 2*n-1: M <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    fb = fa*fa
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fb, M)[:2*n-1]
    # due roundoff small negative
    return float(np.max(conv))
```
But p normalized to sum 1; max c. Score=2n*max. For LSE objective, maybe use same conv.

Let's sketch algorithms in code:

A. `_obj(p, beta)`:
- p normalized? We'll ensure.
- conv = _fft_conv(p)
- If beta large enough, use max. For optimization use exact max to avoid smoothing bias. But exact max not differentiable; for Nelder-Mead okay.
- Return max (score =2n*max). For final choose best exact via evaluate_sequence.

B. `_max_conv(p)`:
- Use np.fft.rfft with length `nfft = 1 << ((2*n-2).bit_length())`.
- `fa = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft); conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, nfft)[:2*n-1]`.
- Due numerical negative, max ignore negatives? Use `conv = np.maximum(conv, 0)`. Return max.
- Maybe for very large n, use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve(p,p, mode='full')`? Our manual ok.

C. `_normalize(p)`: p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return uniform; return p/s. (Scaling invariant but normalization helps optimizer). When p sum 1, score=2n*max. For returned sequence, multiply by 10 maybe.

D. `_objective_theta(theta, n, modes, fft_len, basis)`:
- Compute A = basis @ theta (basis shape (n, 2K)). For numerical stability: A -= A.mean(); p = exp(A); p /= p.sum(). (Subtract mean because adding constant to exp doesn't change after normalization but improves range). Return `_max_conv(p)`.
- Could add small regularization to keep p positive? already.
- If theta large, exp overflows. Clip A to [-50,50]. But basis shape. We can in objective: `A = np.clip(A, -50, 50)`.
- The zero mean of A is subtracted, so average exp not exactly.

But using exponential may bias towards positive perturbations? It can represent f=1+g with g both signs if A both signs after mean subtraction. Good.

E. Low-mode optimizer `_optimize_modes(n, K, n_starts, max_evals, rng, best_state)`:
For each start:
- theta0: random normal scale 0.5 or from height_sequence projection onto basis? We can project log(height) onto basis.
- Use scipy.optimize.minimize with method 'Nelder-Mead', options maxiter=500, xatol=1e-6. Or Powell. Nelder-Mead for moderate dims. We can use `scipy.optimize.minimize(obj, theta0, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': 2000, 'xatol':1e-8, 'fatol':1e-8})`.
- Need be careful with time. We'll allocate time budget.
- Track best p.

However `scipy.optimize.minimize` calls objective many times; each objective allocates A (n x 1) and FFT. For n=4096, 2000 evals ~ maybe 0.5s per start. If many starts/time, okay.

F. Direct local search `_local_search(p0, n, time_limit, rng, initial_M)`:
Use subgradient descent on `L = logsumexp(β c)` with β maybe 20-100. Project to simplex and nonnegative. But can also do multiplicative updates.

Let's design a stable subgradient:
Given p normalized positive. We want minimize f(p)=max c (or LSE). Use gradient of LSE:
- conv = fftconv(p,p)
- weights w = softmax(β conv - β max?) To avoid overflow: `w = np.exp(β*(conv - conv.max())) / sum(...)`.
- grad = ∂LSE/∂p =? LSE = max + (1/β) log ∑e^{β(c_k - max)}. derivative = ∑ w_k ∂c_k/∂p_i.
Need compute grad efficiently.
For each i, grad_i = ∑_k w_k (p_{k-i} + δ_{k=2i} p_i) over valid.
This is cross-correlation of w (length 2N-1) with p. Specifically first part = (w ⊛ p): grad_first[i] = ∑_{k} w_k p_{k-i} for 0≤k-i<N. This is cross-correlation: define w padded length 2N-1; p length N. For i=0..N-1, sum over k=i..i+N-1 w_k p_{k-i}. This is convolution of w with reversed p? We can compute via FFT: `grad_first = ifft( rfft(w) * conj(rfft(p_rev? ))?)`. Simpler: Use `np.convolve(p_rev, w, mode='valid')`? Let's derive:
Let p_rev[j]=p[N-1-j]. Convolve p_rev with w: (p_rev * w)[m] =∑_j p_rev[j] w[m-j] =∑_j p[N-1-j] w[m-j]. For m=N-1+i, terms j? m-j = k, p[N-1-j]=p_i iff j=N-1-i. Then k = N-1+i - (N-1-i)=2i? Not general. Hmm.

def _conv_first(p, w):
    n=len(p)
    m=len(w)
    L=1
    Lreq=n+m-1
    while L<Lreq: L<<=1
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,L)
    return conv[n:2*n][::-1].copy()
```

Now direct PG details. Let's formulate `_projected_gradient_step(p, beta, eta)`:
```
def _projected_gradient_step(p, beta, eta):
    p = np.asarray(p,float); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return uniform
    p = p/s
    conv = _fft_conv_full(p)
    # maybe add tiny to avoid negative; for LSE use conv
    if beta == np.inf? no use finite
    cmax = conv.max()
    shifted = beta*(conv - cmax)
    w = np.exp(shifted - shifted.max()) # shifted max 0; safe
    w = w / w.sum()
    # gradient_first via FFT
    grad_first = _correlate_w_p(w,p)
    diag = w[0::2][:len(p)] * p
    grad = grad_first + diag
    grad -= grad.mean()
    z = p - eta*grad
    newp = _project_simplex(z)
    return newp
```

```
def _local_search(p0, max_iter, deadline, min_improve, tag):
    p = p0.copy(); p = normalize
    f = _max_score(p) # or 2n*max
    it=0; fail=0; eta=0.5? beta=20
    while time<deadline and it<max_iter:
        # maybe adjust beta: increase if stagnation
        pg, f_lse = _projected_gradient_step...
        # line search on pg with eta
        best_p=p; best_f=f
        cur_eta=eta
        for _ in range(6):
            cand = _projected_gradient_step(p, beta, cur_eta)
            fc = _score_internal(cand)
            if fc < best_f - 1e-15:
                best_p=cand; best_f=fc; break? maybe continue lower eta to find smaller step? For efficiency, accept first decrease.
            cur_eta *=0.5
        if improved:
            p=best_p; f=best_f; fail=0; eta *=1.1
            if f < global_best: update
        else:
            fail++; eta*=0.7; if fail>20: beta*=2, fail reset, or break
        it++
    return p
```

Projection to simplex robust:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(v)
    if np.any(~np.isfinite(v)): return uniform
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(n)+1) > (cssv-1))[0][0]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
But sum after projection exactly 1? Numerically almost. We can renormalize: p/(p.sum()) if sum>0.

def _theta_objective(theta, n, K, basis, amplitude=None):
    A = np.zeros(n)
    # maybe dot with basis; basis columns normalized
    A = basis @ theta
    A -= A.mean()
    A = np.clip(A, -50, 50)
    p = np.exp(A)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: return 1e6
    p /= s
    return float(_max_conv_fft(p))
```

```
def _p_from_theta(theta, basis, floor=1e-6):
   y = basis @ theta
   p = 1.0 + y
   if p.min() < floor:
       # shift/scale to nonnegative preserving relative: p = 1 - floor_min + y? But that just adds constant. Better rescale y: alpha = min(1, (1-floor)/max(0,-p.min()))
       y *= alpha
       p = 1 + y
   p = np.maximum(p, floor)
   p /= p.sum()
```

Direct PG details more. Let's implement separate functions:
```
def _full_conv_fft(p):
    n=len(p)
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
    return conv[:2*n-1]
```
We can use same for max. But in PG, need `conv` nonnegative. We can force `conv = np.maximum(conv,0)`. For score max, if negative due roundoff, ignore.

Adam implementation top-level:
```
def _adam_step(theta, m, v, lr, t, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
   m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
   v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
   mh = m/(1-beta1**t); vh=v/(1-beta2**t)
   theta = theta - lr*mh/(np.sqrt(vh)+eps)
   return theta,m,v
```

   norm=norm(grad)

   direction = -grad/norm
   success=False
   for alpha in [max_step, max_step*0.5, max_step*0.25, max_step*0.125]:
       cand=theta+alpha*direction
       val2,_ = _theta_lse_val(cand,basis,beta) # no grad needed? We can compute val only cheaper (no gradθ) but still conv+p. Use _theta_lse_val.
       if val2 < val - 1e-12:

   else: max_step*=0.5; fail+=1; if fail>20: beta*=2; max_step=0.3; fail=0; if beta>5000 break
```

Analytic gradient for θ with softplus:
```
def _theta_function(theta, basis, beta, return_grad):
    n = basis.shape[0]
    y = basis @ theta
    z = 1.0 + y
    # stable softplus: np.logaddexp(0,z)
    base = np.logaddexp(0.0, z)
    s = base.sum()
    p = base / s
    # LSE info
    conv... w...
    val = cmax + log(sum_exp_unnorm)/beta
    if return_grad:
        sig = 1/(1+exp(-z))
        g_p = ... centered invariant
        A = basis.T @ g_p
        B = basis.sum(axis=0)
        Sg = g_p.dot(p)
        grad = (A - Sg * B) / s
        # optionally clip grad finite
    return val, grad
```

PG algorithm details finalized:
```
def _polish(p0, deadline, tag, max_iter=100000, initial_beta=30.0):
    p = _normalize(p0.copy())
    n=len(p); c = _conv_max(p); best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c; f=2*n*c
    beta=initial_beta
    eta=0.5

Detailed function definitions:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
For m=2n-1, if m=1? n=1 ->1. For 3n-2, ok.

`_normalize(p)`:
```
def _normalize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim !=1: p=p.ravel()
    if p.size ==0: return np.array([1.0])
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
       return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64)/p.size if p.size>0 else np.ones(1)/p?
    return p/s
```
If p.size 0, return ones(1). But no.

`_conv_full_fft(p)`:
```
def _conv_full_fft(p):
    n=p.shape[0]
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```

`_score_internal(p)` = 2*n*max(conv). Return float.

`_project_simplex(v)`:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
       v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),0.0,v)
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cssv[?]. Need find rho.
    cond = u * (np.arange(n)+1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond): rho=0 else rho=top
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0: w=np.full(n,1.0/n)
    else: w /= s
    return w
```
The standard projection uses `np.nonzero(u > (cssv-1.0)/(rho+1))[0][0]` alternative. Let's use robust:
```
    rho = np.nonzero(u > (cssv - 1.0) / (np.arange(1, n+1)))[0][0]
```
If n >0. This is standard. Compute `ones=np.arange(1,n+1,float); cond = u > (cssv-1)/ones; rho=...`. If no? At least one. Then theta. Good.

`_lse_grad_and_value(p,beta)`:
```
def _lse_grad_and_value(p,beta):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=p.size
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return ...
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: p=p/s
    L=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(conv,0.0,out=conv)
    cmax=float(conv.max())
    if cmax <=0:
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       return cmax, grad, 0.0
    shift=beta*(conv-cmax)
    wn=np.exp(shift)
    lse=float(cmax + math.log(float(wn.sum()))/float(beta))
    w=wn/wn.sum()
    wrev=np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wrev,L)
    convpw=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,L)
    grad_first=convpw[n:2*n][::-1]
    grad=2.0*grad_first
    grad -= grad.mean()
    return lse, grad, cmax
```
For n=1: L=nextpow2(1)=1; convpw[1:2] empty! Need handle n=1 separately. Our n≥100, but for robustness if n==1: p=[1], conv=[1], grad=[2] centered? Projected gradient sum constraint gradient centered zero; LSE gradient of p^2 at sum1 constrained is zero? Since scale fixed only one point, no movement. Return grad zero. We'll add if n==1.

For LSE gradient, should we return raw grad or centered? For lse objective on simplex, derivative can be centered. We center for PG. For θ gradient formula in `_theta_fun`, using centered also okay; but if criterion invariant to center? Yes we argued adding constant to g yields same θ_grad if formula includes B. If we pass centered g, gradientθ same. Good.

But `_lse_grad_and_value` returns centered grad. In low-dim `_theta_fun`, if we use same function, B term with centered g? The formula invariance to adding constant holds, so OK. However the value LSE computed from p not centered. Good.

`_theta_fun(theta, basis, fft_len, beta)`:
```
def _theta_fun(theta, basis, fft_len, beta):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    y=basis.dot(theta)
    z=1.0+y
    base=np.logaddexp(0.0,z)
    s=float(base.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: return 1e6, zeros
    p=base/s
    lse, grad_p, cmax = _lse_grad_and_value(p, beta)
    # grad_p centered. For normalization derivative:
    A = basis.T.dot(grad_p)
    B = basis.sum(axis=0)  # could precompute but okay
    Sg = float(grad_p.dot(p))
    gradT=(A - Sg*B)/s
    if not finite gradT: gradT=zeros
    return float(lse), gradT
```
But `_lse_grad_and_value` recomputes FFT length maybe 3n-2, not using fft_len. We pass fft_len but not used. Could modify to accept L. To avoid repeated nextpow2. Let's create `_lse_grad_and_value_with_len(p,beta, L)`. Or accept L. Top-level:
```
def _lse_grad_and_value_len(p,beta,L): ...
```
and `_lse_grad_and_value(p,beta)` computes L. `_theta_fun` uses L passed. Basis sum B computed each call; maybe costly but OK. We can pass `basis_sum` to avoid. `_theta_fun(theta, basis, basis_sum, fft_len, beta)`.

Scalar: `base=np.logaddexp(0.0, z)` returns stable. For z large negative, base small; if all z very negative, s could underflow? θ bounds moderate, not.

`_make_basis(n,K)`:
```
t=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
cols=[]
angular = 2.0*np.pi*t/float(n)
for m in range(1,K+1):
    s=np.sin(m*angular); c=np.cos(m*angular)
    # normalize RMS
    ns=np.sqrt(np.mean(s*s)+1e-300); nc=...
    cols.append(s/ns); cols.append(c/nc)
basis=np.column_stack(cols)
return basis
```
Column norm sqrt(n/2) => RMS 1. Good. If n not multiple, sum near zero. B passed.

Could include a low-frequency "bump" basis not zero mean? Since B nonzero, gradient formula handles. But periodic columns zero mean. Add one column `t/(n-1)-0.5` normalized? It has mean -0.5? shift to zero mean. It can create endpoint ramp. But if B=0 (zero mean), formula. Let's add optional first column ramp? Then Fourier modes after. But D increases. Maybe not needed. def _custom_theta_search(theta,basis,basis_sum,L,beta,bounds,max_iter=200):
    theta=np.clip(theta.bounds)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_val,_=_theta_fun(theta,...)
    step=0.3; fail=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       val,grad=_theta_fun(theta,...)
       if val<best_val: best...
       norm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if norm<1e-12: break
       direction=-grad/norm
       improved=False
       for alpha in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]:
          cand=np.clip(theta+alpha*direction, low, high)
          val2,_=_theta_fun(cand,...) # or val only? _theta_fun returns grad
          if val2<val:
             theta=cand; val=val2; improved=True; break
       if improved: step=min(1.0,step*1.1)
       else: step*=0.5; fail++; if fail>20: break
    return best_theta
```

Implementation:
```
def _polish(p0, deadline, rng, tag=''):
    p=_normalize(p0.copy())
    n=p.size
    c=_conv_max(p) if hasattr? but _conv_max returns max not convolution. Use _score? Let's implement `_max_conv(p)`.
    best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c; best_score=2*n*c
    beta=30.0
    eta=1.0
    fail=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 1000000:
        lse, grad, c_now = _lse_grad_and_value(p,beta)
        if c_now < best_c: best update (maybe p already)
        normg=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if normg < 1e-14: break
        improved=False
        for alpha in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.125, eta*0.0625):
            z = p - alpha*grad
            cand = _project_simplex(z)
            cc = _max_conv(cand)
            if cc + 1e-15 < best_c:
                best_p=cand; best_c=cc; p=cand; improved=True; break
        if improved:
            fail=0
            eta = min(5.0, eta*1.03)
            it +=1
        else:
            fail +=1
            eta *=0.7
            if fail > 40:
                beta = min(2000.0, beta*1.5)
                eta = 0.5
                fail=0
                if beta > 2000:
                    # random kick
                    kick = np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,n))
                    cand=_normalize(p*kick)
                    if _max_conv(cand) < best_c: best...
                    if rng.random()<0.1:
                       sym=0.5*(p+p[::-1]); symc=_max_conv(sym); if symc<best...
                    break
                continue
            # occasional symmetry
            if it % 50 ==0:
               sym=0.5*(p+p[::-1]); sc=_max_conv(sym); if sc<best_c: ...
    return best_p, 2*n*best_c
```
But if `fail>40` and beta increase, `continue` recompute grad. If eta becomes very small (say <1e-6), set fail. Good.
`_max_conv` here returns max only; score=2n*max. We can define `_score_internal` calling it.

```

Now, let's code `_safe_project`:
```
def _safe_project(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.array([1.0])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
       v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),0.0,v)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s <=0:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    if np.min(v) >= -1e-12:
       w=np.where(v<0.0,0.0,v)
       s=float(w.sum())
       if s >0:
          w = w/s
          return w
    return _project_simplex(v)
```
This uses np.min O(n). OK.

`_project_simplex`: Use `np.arange` each call; can be costly but okay. Optimize with static? Not needed.

Let's verify projection formula:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ones=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    rho=np.nonzero(u > (cssv - 1.0)/ones)[0][0]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
    else: w=np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return w
```
Standard. For v sum1 and nonnegative, `_safe_project` avoids this.

Let's detail `_make_basis`:
```
def _make_basis(n, K):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ang = (2.0*math.pi*t)/float(n)
    cols=[]
    for m in range(1,K+1):
        s=np.sin(m*ang)
        c=np.cos(m*ang)
        ns=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s*s))) if n>0 else 1.0
        nc=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(c*c)))
        if ns>1e-300: s=s/ns
        if nc>1e-300: c=c/nc
        cols.append(s); cols.append(c)
    if not cols:
       return np.zeros((n,0))
    return np.column_stack(cols)
```
K always >0.

`_pad_theta`:
```
def _pad_theta(theta, D):
    x=np.zeros(D,float)
    if theta is not None:
       m=min(D,len(theta))
       x[:m]=theta[:m]
    return x
```

`_clip_theta`:

def _clip_theta(theta): return np.clip(theta, -3.0, 3.0)

`_theta_fun` uses `basis_sum` precomputed. Need `_lse_grad_and_value_len` not compute nextpower. We'll implement:
```
def _lse_grad_and_value_len(p,beta,L):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=p.size
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return 0, zeros, 0
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: p=p/s
    if n==1:
       val=float(p[0]*p[0])
       grad=np.zeros(1,float)
       return val, grad, val
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
    conv=conv[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(conv,0.0,out=conv)
    cmax=float(conv.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n), 0.0
    shift=beta*(conv-cmax)
    wn=np.exp(shift)
    wsum=float(wn.sum())
    if wsum <=0 or not finite: val=cmax; w=np.full(2*n-1, 1/(2n-1))
    else: val=cmax + math.log(wsum)/beta; w=wn/wsum
    wrev=np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
    fw=np.fft.rfft(wrev,L)
    convpw=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,L)
    grad_first=convpw[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    grad=2.0*grad_first
    grad -= grad.mean()
    return float(val), grad, cmax
```
If n=1, L? not.

`_theta_fun`:
```
def _theta_fun(theta,basis,basis_sum,L,beta):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    y=basis.dot(theta)
    z=1.0+y
    base=np.logaddexp(0.0,z)
    s=float(base.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return 1e6, np.zeros_like(theta)
    p=base/s
    val, grad_p, _ = _lse_grad_and_value_len(p,beta,L)
    A=basis.T.dot(grad_p)
    Sg=float(grad_p.dot(p))
    gradT=(A - Sg*basis_sum)/s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gradT)):
        gradT=np.zeros_like(theta)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e6
    return float(val), gradT.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
`basis_sum` shape D. Good.

`_theta_val` for custom search? We can just call `_theta_fun` and use val. If SciPy absent, many grads computed; okay.

def _custom_theta_search(theta,basis,basis_sum,L,beta,bounds,max_iter=200):
    low=np.array([b[0] for b in bounds]) # no lambda? list comprehension okay? It's not lambda. But can use numpy arrays.
    high=...
```
No lambda. `bounds` list of tuples. Use:
```
low = np.array([b[0] for b in bounds], dtype=float)
high = np.array([b[1] for b in bounds], dtype=float)
```
List comprehension is fine (not lambda).
```
    theta=np.clip(np.asarray(theta,float),low,high)

           if sc<best_score: ...

    for _ in range(20): ...

```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function search for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program works entirely with normalized sequences p (sum(p) == 1).
It combines:
  * quick one-mode sine seeds,
  * low-dimensional Fourier/softplus parameter optimization (L-BFGS-B),
  * high-dimensional projected-gradient polishing with a log-sum-exp
    surrogate for the max-convolution term,
  * exact final re-evaluation of shortlisted candidates.

All candidates are returned with sum 1, so clipping in the evaluator
never triggers and scaling remains invariant.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from operator import itemgetter

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    return p


def _conv_full_fft(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _max_conv(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = _conv_full_fft(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(c.max())


def _score_internal(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    return 2.0 * float(p.size) * _max_conv(p)


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), 0.0, v)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ones = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    idx = np.nonzero(u > (cssv - 1.0) / ones)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        rho = 0
    else:
        rho = int(idx[0])

    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _safe_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), 0.0, v)

    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    if float(v.min()) >= -1e-12:
        w = np.where(v < 0.0, 0.0, v)
        s2 = float(w.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            w = w / s2
            return w

    return _project_simplex(v)


def _lse_grad_and_value_len(p, beta, L):
    """
    Return (LSE objective, gradient wrt p, max coefficient).
    p is normalized internally.  The FFT length L must be >= 3*n-2.
    """
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 1:
        val = float(p[0] * p[0])
        return val, np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64), val

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)
    conv = conv[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(conv, 0.0, out=conv)

    cmax = float(conv.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    shift = float(beta) * (conv - cmax)
    wn = np.exp(shift)
    wsum = float(wn.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
        val = cmax
        w = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        val = cmax + math.log(wsum) / float(beta)
        w = wn / wsum

    wrev = np.ascontiguousarray(w[::-1])
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wrev, L)
    convpw = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, L)

    # grad_p[i] = 2 * sum_k w[k] * p[k-i] over valid k.
    grad_first = convpw[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    grad = 2.0 * grad_first
    grad -= grad.mean()

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False), cmax


def _make_basis(n, K):
    n = int(n)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.zeros((n, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ang = (2.0 * math.pi * t) / float(n)
    cols = []

    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        s = np.sin(float(m) * ang)
        c = np.cos(float(m) * ang)
        ns = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(s * s)))
        nc = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(c * c)))
        if ns > 1e-300:
            s = s / ns
        if nc > 1e-300:
            c = c / nc
        cols.append(s)
        cols.append(c)

    return np.column_stack(cols)


def _p_from_basis(theta, basis):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = basis.dot(theta)
    z = 1.0 + y
    base = np.logaddexp(0.0, z)
    s = float(base.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(basis.shape[0], 1.0 / max(1, basis.shape[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    p = base / s
    return p


def _p_from_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = 1.0 + y
    base = np.logaddexp(0.0, z)
    s = float(base.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / max(1, y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return base / s


def _theta_fun(theta, basis, basis_sum, L, beta):
    """
    Objective + gradient for the low-dimensional softplus Fourier model.
    """
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(basis.shape[0])
    D = int(basis.shape[1]) if basis.ndim == 2 else 0

    if D == 0:
        return 1e6, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    y = basis.dot(theta)
    z = 1.0 + y
    base = np.logaddexp(0.0, z)
    s = float(base.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e6, np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)

    p = base / s
    val, grad_p, _ = _lse_grad_and_value_len(p, beta, L)

    sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z))
    gbase = sig * grad_p

    A = basis.T.dot(gbase)
    Sg = float(grad_p.dot(p))
    gradT = (A - Sg * basis_sum) / s

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gradT)):
        gradT = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6

    return float(val), gradT.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _make_bounds(D):
    return [(-3.0, 3.0) for _ in range(D)]


def _pad_theta(theta, D):
    x = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta is not None and len(theta) > 0:
        m = min(D, len(theta))
        x[:m] = theta[:m]
    return np.clip(x, -3.0, 3.0)


def _custom_theta_search(theta, basis, basis_sum, L, beta, bounds, max_iter=200):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    low = np.array([b[0] for b in bounds], dtype=np.float64)
    high = np.array([b[1] for b in bounds], dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.clip(theta, low, high)

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_val, _ = _theta_fun(theta, basis, basis_sum, L, beta)

    step = 0.3
    fail = 0

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        val, grad = _theta_fun(theta, basis, basis_sum, L, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            break
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_theta = theta.copy()

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-12:
            break

        direction = -grad / gnorm
        improved = False

        for alpha in (step, step * 0.5, step * 0.25, step * 0.125):
            cand = np.clip(theta + alpha * direction, low, high)
            val2, _ = _theta_fun(cand, basis, basis_sum, L, beta)
            if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < val - 1e-15:
                theta = cand
                val = val2
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            step = min(1.0, step * 1.05)
            fail = 0
        else:
            step *= 0.5
            fail += 1
            if fail > 25:
                break

    return best_theta


def _sine_grid(n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return None, float("inf")

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    modes = (1, 2, 3)
    amps = (0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0)
    phases = (0.0, 0.5 * math.pi, math.pi, -0.5 * math.pi)

    for m in modes:
        for amp in amps:
            for phase in phases:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    return best_p, best_score
                y = amp * np.sin((2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * t) / float(n) + phase)
                p = _p_from_y(y)
                sc = _score_internal(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_p = p.copy()

    for _ in range(24):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        m = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 1.05))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        y = amp * np.sin((2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * t) / float(n) + phase)
        p = _p_from_y(y)
        sc = _score_internal(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_p = p.copy()

    return best_p, best_score


def _modes_optimize(n, max_K, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 8:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    base_theta = None

    K_values = []
    for K in (1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12):
        if K <= max_K:
            K_values.append(K)
        if len(K_values) >= 5 and K >= max_K:
            break

    for K in K_values:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break

        D = 2 * K
        basis = _make_basis(n, K)
        basis_sum = basis.sum(axis=0)
        L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        beta = 30.0 + 8.0 * float(K)

        starts = []

        if base_theta is not None:
            x = _pad_theta(base_theta, D)
            x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, D), -3.0, 3.0)
            starts.append(x)

        if K == 1:
            for a in (0.25, 0.45, 0.75):
                x = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
                x[0] = a
                starts.append(x)
            x = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
            x[1] = 0.35
            starts.append(x)
        else:
            for _ in range(2):
                x = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, D)
                starts.append(np.clip(x, -3.0, 3.0))

        maxiter = 70 + 18 * int(K)
        bounds = _make_bounds(D)

        for st in starts:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break

            st = np.asarray(st, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            st = np.clip(st, -3.0, 3.0)

            if _scipy_minimize is not None:
                try:
                    res = _scipy_minimize(
                        _theta_fun,
                        st,
                        args=(basis, basis_sum, L, beta),
                        method="L-BFGS-B",
                        bounds=bounds,
                        options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-10, "maxls": 50},
                    )
                    theta = res.x
                except Exception:
                    theta = _custom_theta_search(
                        st, basis, basis_sum, L, beta, bounds, max_iter=180
                    )
            else:
                theta = _custom_theta_search(
                    st, basis, basis_sum, L, beta, bounds, max_iter=180
                )

            p = _p_from_basis(theta, basis)
            sc = _score_internal(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    base_theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    print(f"[_modes_optimize] n={n} K={K} internal={sc:.8f}")

        if base_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            st = _pad_theta(base_theta, D)
            beta2 = min(250.0, beta * 2.2)

            if _scipy_minimize is not None:
                try:
                    res = _scipy_minimize(
                        _theta_fun,
                        st,
                        args=(basis, basis_sum, L, beta2),
                        method="L-BFGS-B",
                        bounds=bounds,
                        options={"maxiter": max(60, maxiter), "ftol": 1e-11, "maxls": 50},
                    )
                    theta = res.x
                except Exception:
                    theta = _custom_theta_search(
                        st, basis, basis_sum, L, beta2, bounds, max_iter=160
                    )
            else:
                theta = _custom_theta_search(
                    st, basis, basis_sum, L, beta2, bounds, max_iter=160
                )

            p = _p_from_basis(theta, basis)
            sc = _score_internal(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    base_theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    print(f"[_modes_optimize] n={n} K={K} high-beta internal={sc:.8f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def _polish(p0, deadline, rng, tag=""):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    if p.size == 0:
        return p, float("inf")

    n = int(p.size)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = _max_conv(p)
    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_c

    beta = 30.0
    eta = 1.0
    fail = 0
    it = 0

    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 1000000:
        if best_score <= 1.5000:
            break

        _, grad, c_now = _lse_grad_and_value_len(p, beta, L)

        if np.isfinite(c_now) and c_now < best_c:
            best_c = float(c_now)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_c

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        improved = False
        for alpha in (eta, eta * 0.5, eta * 0.25, eta * 0.125, eta * 0.0625):
            z = p - alpha * grad
            cand = _safe_project(z)
            cc = _max_conv(cand)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc + 1e-15 < best_c:
                best_p = cand.copy()
                best_c = float(cc)
                best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_c
                p = cand
                improved = True
                break

        it += 1

        if improved:
            fail = 0
            eta = min(5.0, eta * 1.04)
        else:
            fail += 1
            eta *= 0.7
            if fail > 45:
                if beta >= 2000.0:
                    break
                beta = min(2000.0, beta * 1.4)
                eta = 0.6
                fail = 0

                # Small stochastic kicks can escape flat/kink regions.
                if rng.random() < 0.35:
                    kick = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n))
                    cand = _normalize(p * kick)
                    cc = _max_conv(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                        best_p = cand.copy()
                        best_c = float(cc)
                        best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_c
                        p = cand

                sym = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
                sym = _normalize(sym)
                sc = _max_conv(sym)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_c:
                    best_p = sym.copy()
                    best_c = float(sc)
                    best_score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_c
                    p = sym

    print(f"[_polish] {tag} n={n} internal={best_score:.8f}")
    return best_p, best_score


def _consider(gallery, p, score, tag):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = _normalize(p)
    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    gallery.append((score, p.copy(), tag))
    gallery.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    if len(gallery) > 10:
        gallery.pop()


def _candidate_to_list(p):
    p = _normalize(p)
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))

    final_reserve = min(120.0, max(5.0, budget * 0.05))
    if final_reserve >= 0.8 * budget:
        final_reserve = max(5.0, 0.1 * budget)
    master_deadline = start + max(1.0, budget - final_reserve)

    gallery = []

    # Reliable fallback: constant sequence has score exactly 2 in exact arithmetic.
    fallback_n = 1024
    fallback_p = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(fallback_n)
    _consider(gallery, fallback_p, 2.0, "constant_fallback")
    print(f"[init] constant fallback internal=2.0")

    # Try seeding from the previously found sequence if available.
    height_p = None
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            h = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            if h.size >= 20 and h.size <= 16384 and float(h.sum()) > 0.0:
                height_p = _normalize(h)
                sc = _score_internal(height_p)
                _consider(gallery, height_p, sc, "height_sequence_1")
                print(f"[init] height_sequence_1 n={height_p.size} internal={sc:.8f}")
        except Exception:
            height_p = None

    # Seeds and quick polishing.
    quick_deadline = min(master_deadline, start + 90.0)
    for n in (256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192):
        if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline:
            break
        dl = min(quick_deadline, time.monotonic() + 12.0)
        p, sc = _sine_grid(n, dl, rng)
        if p is not None:
            _consider(gallery, p, sc, f"sine_{n}")

    if height_p is not None and time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 90.0:
        dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        p, sc = _polish(height_p, dl, rng, tag="height_quick")
        _consider(gallery, p, sc, "height_quick_polished")

    if gallery and time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 120.0:
        p0 = gallery[0][1]
        dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        p, sc = _polish(p0, dl, rng, tag="quick_best")
        _consider(gallery, p, sc, "quick_best_polished")

    # Main structured optimization at a medium size.
    if time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 600.0:
        n_main = 4096
        modes_dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 540.0)
        p, sc = _modes_optimize(n_main, 6, modes_dl, rng)
        if p is not None:
            _consider(gallery, p, sc, f"modes_{n_main}")

        if time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 150.0:
            p0 = gallery[0][1]
            dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 360.0)
            p, sc = _polish(p0, dl, rng, tag=f"polish_{n_main}")
            _consider(gallery, p, sc, f"polished_{n_main}")

    # Larger instance if there is still meaningful time.
    if time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 520.0:
        n_large = 8192
        modes_dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 430.0)
        p, sc = _modes_optimize(n_large, 8, modes_dl, rng)
        if p is not None:
            _consider(gallery, p, sc, f"modes_{n_large}")

        if time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 150.0:
            p0 = gallery[0][1]
            dl = min(master_deadline, time.monotonic() + 320.0)
            p, sc = _polish(p0, dl, rng, tag=f"polish_{n_large}")
            _consider(gallery, p, sc, f"polished_{n_large}")

    # Final continuous polish of the current best.
    if gallery and time.monotonic() < master_deadline - 30.0:
        p0 = gallery[0][1]
        p, sc = _polish(p0, master_deadline, rng, tag="final_best")
        _consider(gallery, p, sc, "final_best_polished")

    # Exact re-evaluation of the best internal candidates.  Keep this short
    # because propose_candidate must return before the hard deadline.
    best_seq = _candidate_to_list(fallback_p)
    best_exact = _exact_score(best_seq)

    checked = 0
    for score, p, tag in gallery:
        if time.monotonic() > master_deadline + final_reserve * 0.5:
            break
        if checked >= 6:
            break
        seq = _candidate_to_list(p)
        es = _exact_score(seq)
        checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
            best_exact = es
            best_seq = seq
            print(f"[final] improved exact score via {tag}: {es:.8f}")

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_seq = _candidate_to_list(fallback_p)
        best_exact = _exact_score(best_seq)

    print(f"[done] best exact score = {best_exact:.8f}")
    return best_seq
```
